### Recommendation (plan §8.6)

**pdfium.fixed-128-o15.nohdr.te3l-256+weighted0.3** (run `sweep-20260930T185815-b8c2.s4.f6e3c8601c`): recall @ 2k 0.977 [0.950-0.996], p95 local retrieval 9.1 ms.

- latency filter: Pi p95 local retrieval <= 300 ms (S6); configurations not measured on the Pi are not eligible
- highest budgeted recall @ 2k on dev: pdfium.fixed-128-o15.nohdr.te3l-256+weighted0.3 (0.977)

- abstain threshold: not calibrated (the generation-side NOT_IN_SOURCES reply decides)
